The booked text says 'TWO at schema 3'; that predated the 3->4 bump and now also the 4->5 bump this wave landed (lifecycle 370e24e, dotfiles ebd7f41, both verified by my own read of each declaration and all three carriers rather than from a report), and lc-179's 5->6 will move it again before this wave closes. A reader RE-DERIVES the floor from declaration.SCHEMA_FLOOR and never cites a figure from this slot - a stated total and its enumeration part company without a symptom, which is why the direction, not the digit, is what this entry now carries. THE DIRECTION AND THE EIGHT ARE UNCHANGED and were re-confirmed at the artifact: ten declarations, two at floor, eight at 2. NEW CLASS FACT, and this entry explicitly recorded it as unmeasured ('NOT MEASURED: whether any of the eight carries further below-floor residue beyond the trigger stage'): running migrate --schema-from as a DRY RUN over all ten, twice, skill-craft answers COULD NOT VERIFY exactly as dispatch-guards does - so TWO of the eight need more than a stamp bump, not one. The dispatch-guards case was relayed from the driving desk and carried as theirs; the skill-craft case is measured here. The other six answer FINDING [migration_unclassified], which is the ordinary below-floor state this entry describes. NOTHING WAS WRITTEN to any of the eight: each repo's working-tree state was hashed before and after every run and compared, all ten NO-WRITE on both passes, which is how this entry's MUST-NOT-MOVE (the eight are not touched while their own sessions hold them) was honoured by construction rather than by care.

## lc-240
grade: PARKED
requirement: RETRIEVAL IS ALL-OR-NOTHING AND SHOULD BE TWO-STAGE, AND DECISIONS ARE INDEXED BY NOTHING. A session either ingests an artifact wholly at start or never reaches for it, so the environment's only lever today is loading MORE, which is the reader-stops-reading disease. The operator's shape instead: a lean always-on INDEX whose entry (a tool exists that relates to my task) triggers a LOOK, where the look supplies both the pattern and any recorded prior decision, the model's own relevance judgment does the matching, and the environment only keeps the index in view. THE GAP BEYOND THE INDEX ITSELF, and it is the part nothing here covers: DECISIONS INDEXED BY THE ARTIFACT THEY CONCERN. The ledger holds decisions chronologically and nothing surfaces the fact that an operator decision exists about a thing shaped like the task in hand. This is a GENERALIZATION rather than an invention: three local instances already exist (the runbook router table, kind list --digest, and the structure line lc-174 shipped), and the round decides the shape.
goal: enforce-the-invariants
write-set: UNKNOWN
done-criterion: UNKNOWN pending the round, deliberately: the round holds the design and this entry must not pre-empt it. What is FIXED as the shape the design answers to: an index entry is a DESCRIPTION rather than the content, its absence for a relevant artifact is the under-trigger failure, its presence for an irrelevant one is the over-trigger failure, and its divergence from the body it describes is the drift failure. The design names which of the three it detects and how, or says which it does not. MUST-NOT-MOVE: the environment keeps the index in view and never does the matching. The operator's own statement is that the model's relevance judgment does that, so a mechanism that decided relevance would be replacing the judgment this arc exists to feed rather than feeding it.
evidence: OPERATOR-STATED 2026-09-19, first-hand at the driving desk cachyos-setup-b3 and RELAYED here, carried as theirs and not re-measured at this desk. THE WORKED SPECIMEN, their account: the Marvel arc built tool one auto-starting with the game, a taste ruling stated once; tool two was then built NOT auto-starting. Their words, near-verbatim: made no sense, it never looked, it didn't even need my prior decision, if it had looked at the existing tool judgment would have been pretty clear. THEIR OWN NAMING OF THE MECHANISM, near-verbatim: like MCP or skills, they carry descriptions, not the whole corpus, and get loaded when relevant. DERIVED at the driving desk, three consequences. (1) AN INDEX ENTRY IS A SKILL DESCRIPTION, and description quality already has an eval discipline: skill-craft's Tier-1 triggering evals, blind router simulation with under and over-trigger grading, transfer directly to grading index entries. The round inherits an INSTRUMENT rather than only a pattern, which is rare for anything on its agenda. (2) THE FAILURE MODES TRANSFER TOO: under-trigger, which is the Marvel case since no entry existed; over-trigger, which is index noise and the reader-stops-reading disease; and description-body DRIFT, which is the label-over-body class. Lifecycle is better armed than the skill system on the third, because a kind carries a staleness stage and a skill listing does not. (3) THREE LOCAL INSTANCES ALREADY EXIST, so this is a generalization: the runbook router table, kind list --digest (lc-219), and the structure line lc-174 shipped and the driving desk wired into the session banner at dotfiles 9ef268d. MEASURED at this desk, the one thing verified here rather than relayed: that third instance is live, since session-scan.py:239 calls kind list --structure and renders its lines. DERIVED, placement: this is purpose.md's presence-as-selection made concrete, and it is the corpus's read-the-existing-instances rule made environmental rather than remembered. NOT MEASURED ANYWHERE: no count exists of how often a session failed to reach for an artifact it held, which is the same unbuilt measurement docs/answerable-not-felt.md names when it withdraws the retrieval grade.
blocked-by: decision has the design round closed, settling whether the index is a new kind, a widened digest, or a declaration field?
